- **builder**: Mid-tier implementer for well-scoped changes with a clear spec: apply a planned edit, write a test from a described behavior, mechanical refactors, doc/report updates. - **builder**: Implements one issue from a plan or spec in the worktree it is given, pushes, and opens or updates the PR. Use for any feature, bug fix, or refactor that has a written spec. - **builder**: Primary implementer on Opus — carries out the substantive, judgement-requiring steps of an approved plan: non-trivial logic, wiring that needs decisions, anything where the "how" is not fully spelled out. Use for the parts a mechanical Sonnet implementer would get wrong. It writes code, but does NOT re-open the design. For purely mechanical steps (rote renames, applying a settled pattern) use the Sonnet `implementer` instead. Starts fresh with no conversation history — give it the plan step, the design intent, and the relevant file paths. ([marlls1989/claude-tiered-development](https://github.com/marlls1989/claude-tiered-development/blob/HEAD/agents/builder.md))

- **business-analyst**: Бизнес-аналитик: разбирает текущий процесс (AS-IS), проектирует целевой (TO-BE), пишет BRD с бизнес-требованиями и правилами, Use Case, Gap-анализ и матрицу трассируемости.
